2026. 11.2
先週（10/26～10/30）発表の主な経済指標
AI関連を中心に設備投資が増勢を維持し、産業用機械にも広
がり。好調な企業収益も投資を後押し。個人消費も堅調。金
融所得や移転所得、株高による資産効果が購買力を下支え
政策要因を除く都区部CPIは上昇基調。財価格の上昇が目立
つ一方、サービス価格改定の広がりは限定的。企業の価格転嫁
は進展も、消費マインドの弱さが値上げの重荷に
注目点② 日本で進む価格転嫁、節約志向との綱引きが続く注目点①米国経済は投資の裾野拡大と消費の強さを確認
7～9月期GDPは成⾧加速。AI関連を中心とする設
備投資の拡大に加え、個人消費も堅調推移。9月
のコアPCE物価は3％を超える伸びが継続。景気の
底堅さを背景にFRBにもう一段の金融引き締め余地

労働分配率は低下傾向。賃金
以外の所得が家計所得を補完
米国：企業収益が拡大する一方、労働分配率は低下。金融所得や移転所得が消費を下支え
 企業収益が好調を維持する一方、労働所得の対GDP比は低下傾向。金融所得や政府からの移転所得が家計所得を補完
― 株式配当や社会保障給付など、賃金以外の所得が家計の購買力を支える構図
 こうした所得環境に加え、株高による資産効果も消費を下支え。10月入り後も個人消費は底堅く推移
― 燃料高にもかかわらず、空港利用者数は前年並みの水準を維持し、小売売上高も高めの伸びを維持
― 年末商戦も前年を上回る伸びが予想されている

日本：コアCPIは10月に鈍化も、政策要因を除けばインフレ率は上昇基調
4
 10月の都区部コアＣＰＩは前年比＋2.6％（前月：同＋2.7％）と伸び鈍化
― 無償化期間の時期ズレによる9月水道料金の一時的な押し上げが剥落したことに加え、コメ価格の下落が影響
 ただし、政策要因を除けばインフレ率は上昇基調。サービス物価が底堅く推移する中、財物価の加速がインフレ率押し上げ
― 原油高等によるコスト増の転嫁が進展し、エネルギー、その他財（除く水道料金）の価格上昇が足元で加速
― 春先に鈍化した一般サービス物価（除く家賃）も人件費等の転嫁進展を映じて前年比＋2％近傍で推移

―10月は外食を中心に価格改定の動きがみられるも、広がりは限定的。一方、外食除く改定品目では値上げ幅が拡大
（注） 食品は、コメと外食を除く食料価格。水道料金はその他財に該当。サービスは一般
サービスと公共サービスの合計（家賃を除く）
（出所） 総務省「消費者物価指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

日本：企業の価格転嫁は進展も、消費マインドの弱さが追加値上げの重荷に
5
 企業の価格転嫁は着実に進展
― 日商LOBO調査では、コスト増加分の4割以上を価格転嫁できた企業が55.4％と4月から2.6％ポイント増加。ただし、労
務費の転嫁は40.6％にとどまり、販売単価DIも小幅低下するなど、中小企業では追加値上げの勢いに一服感
 消費者態度指数は2カ月連続で悪化。消費マインドの弱さは残存
― 物価高による節約志向が企業の追加値上げを制約している可能性。日銀は基調的な物価上昇率が2％に到達したと判
断し、上振れリスクへの警戒を強化。ただし、サービス価格の改定に広がりはみられず
（出所） 日本商工会議所「LOBO調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（注） 二人以上世帯、季節調整値。シャドー部分は景気後退局面
（出所） 内閣府「消費動向調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 7

米国：10月FOMC～据え置き、ウォーシュ議長は引き締めがAIセクターにも影響との認識示す
6
 3.75～4.00％に据え置き（2人が反対、利上げを支持）。景気評価を上方修正しつつ、9月利上げ効果を見極め
― 景気評価は「堅調」から「力強い」へ。ウォーシュ議長は投資の広がりに言及
 AIセクターでは投資の選別が起きており、高い成長期待を持つ同セクターに対しても金融政策は有効との認識示す
 2027・2028年の会合の一部を政策決定を行わず、声明文も公表しない戦略的議論の会合とすると表明
AIセクター、生産性、タスクフォース声明文の変更、政策判断、会合運営
 反対2票は利上げを主張
 景気評価は「堅調」から「力強い」へ
 様子見による「情報のさらなる蓄積」
が、物価安定への「適時の復帰」のため
の政策判断に役立つ
声明文
 「以前より引き締まった」が「引き締め
的な水準にあるわけではない」と述べ、
追加利上げの余地残す
 「参加者の多数派は利上げ効果が出てい
ると認識」と言及

金融環境
と効果
 「政治は、この決定に何の役割も果たし
ていない」と言明 中間選挙
 2027年秋の1回、2028年には2回の会合
を政策決定せず議論だけの会合へ
 戦略的な熟議のための時間と余地確保
会合運営
 「投資家が期待する収益率はあまりに違
うので、AIには何の影響も及ばない」と
いう見方を否定
 「新技術と今回の投資急増」に対して金
融政策は有効
 信用スプレッドの拡大などは「市場参加
者が、どのような投資を行いたいか、資
本配分を選別している」ことの表れ
AI投資
 「経済全体の生産性は、ここ2、3年ほど
上向いて」おり、高い経済成⾧率によっ
てインフレ圧力が生まれ、利上げが必要
な状況ではないとの認識
生産性
11月16日に生産性・雇用の公聴会。タスク
フォース報告は年末が目安（一部は遅延の
可能性）。バランスシート提言は2027年1
～3月期の早期を想定
タスク
フォース

### Page 8

内容ポイント
 26年3Qの景気は引き続き拡大
 エネルギー価格と資金調達コストの上昇が今後の重しに景気
 エネルギー価格の見通しは9月の予想を上回っている
 今後数四半期の物価は目標を大幅に上回る見通し。その後
はエネルギー価格低下と金利上昇により目標水準に回帰へ
 物価の基調・賃金はおおむね安定。ただし、価格形成の初
期段階では上昇圧力がみられる
 今後6週間で得られる、基調的なインフレに関する多くの情
報を、とりわけ注視していく
物価
 9月理事会以降、金融環境は引き締まっている。金利は上昇
し、株価は下落している
 期待インフレの上昇やハイパースケーラーの社債発行等、
複合的な要因が金利を押し上げ。加えて、債務の多い加盟
国は固有の課題に直面
 市場の不当かつ無秩序な動向が金融政策伝達の妨げとな
る場合、対応手段は用意されている
金融
環境
 全会一致で金利据え置きも、利上げが適切かどうか議論し
た。「今利上げを実施してはどうか」との意見もあった
 今後の金融政策はデータに基づき、会合ごとに決定

政策

金利水準 運転資金・在庫

（「増加」－「減少」、％）
欧州：ECBは政策金利を据え置き。高金利は企業の資金需要を押し下げ
7
 ECBは預金ファシリティ金利を2.5％で据え置き。利上げが議論されるも物価の基調に著変なく、慎重な対応が適切と判断
― 利上げの要否検討にあたり、エネルギー価格の上昇と金融環境の引き締まりが主な論点に。今後は物価の基調に関す
る指標を特に注視する姿勢。ラガルド総裁は、進むべき方向が「きわめて明確」であると発言
― ラガルド総裁は2027年2月に早期退任へ。同年5月までに専務理事2名を含む計3名の幹部が交代する見込み
 10月の銀行貸出調査によれば、金利水準が企業の資金需要減少に寄与。高金利の影響は既に一部で顕在化
― 同調査によれば、企業向け貸出基準はフランスで一段と厳格化。銀行のリスク許容度低下やリスク認識の高まりが主因

企業向け銀行貸出の需要増減要因
（注） 需要増減は、企業向け貸出の需要が前四半期から「かなり増加」「やや増加」と回
答した銀行の割合から、「かなり減少」「やや減少」と回答した銀行の割合を引いた
もの。「その他要因」には他の調達手段による代替や、「運転資金・在庫」「設備投
資」以外の資金需要発生等を含む。2026年Q3の調査期間は9月21日～10月6日
（出所）ECBより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
10月ECB理事会のポイント（声明文・総裁会見）
（出所）ECBより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
金利水準の高さが
貸出需要を下押し

### Page 9

英国：政府は財政規律を重視しつつエネルギー高対策に注力。財政余力は縮小
 英政府は秋季財政計画を公表。財政規律を重視しつつ家計や小規模事業者向け支援等を打ち出す内容
― 燃料税減税と電気料金へのVAT減税の延長、給付金支給によるエネルギー高対策、住宅購入支援などの家計支援策
を発表。小規模事業者の税負担軽減や電気料金の引き下げなど、企業向け支援も公表
― 上記政策の財源は銀行への追加課税やギャンブルへの課税強化等で確保する方針
― 防衛費は2030年までにGDP比3％へ引き上げる方針。ただし、財源を含む詳細は来年の歳出見直しに先送り
 財政余力は前回3月の237億ポンドから147億ポンドに大幅縮小。中東情勢悪化に伴う金利上昇等が背景
英国：財政余力（左）と3月以降の財政余力減少要因（右）
（出所）OBRより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
英国：秋季財政計画の主な内容
（出所） 英国政府より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
• 燃料税5ペンス減税や電気料金のVA T免除を来年7月まで実施
• 2,000 万世帯を対象に100ポンドのエネルギー高支援給付金支給

• 使用量にかかわらず課される家庭用ガスのスタンディングチャー
ジ廃止
• エネルギー集約型製造業1万社の電気代を20～25％軽減
• 手ごろな住宅建設に15億ポンド追加拠出。住宅初回購入者向け
支援「Your First Home」に47億ポンドを充当
• 小規模事業者の事業用不動産税免除対象を拡大。パブや音楽会
場等には同税を追加で20％軽減
• 食品等100件以上の品目の関税撤廃
• 防衛投資計画で必要とされていた150億ポンドの追加投資全額を
拠出。2030年に防衛費GDP比3％達成を企図。来年の歳出見直
しで財源の詳細公表
• 地方分権の一環として、来年から事業用不動産税のうち20％を地
方に移転、2030年までに100％への引き上げを目指すほか、所得
税も2028年から一部を地方に配分しインフラ整備の財源に
• 銀行への上乗せ課税を3％から6％に引き上げ
• 高額賭博機へのギャンブル税を20％から25％に引き上げ
• 貯蓄・不動産所得の税率を2％Pt引き上げ

英国：財政懸念を背景とする金利上昇リスクはくすぶり続ける見通し
 財政計画がおおむね事前の予想どおりの内容であったことなどから英10年債利回りの動きは限定的。ただし、足元の高金
利の要因であるインフレや財政懸念、世界的なAI関連投資増加は今後も続くとみられ、金利は高止まりが予想される

 利払い費による財政圧迫継続が予想される中、財政懸念を背景とする一段の金利上昇リスクはくすぶり続ける見通し
― 高金利継続による利払い負担が財政を圧迫。財政を巡る不確実性が高い状況は続く見通し
― 欧州各国では財政懸念や政情不安を背景に高債務国中心に国債利回り上昇
― 財政への懸念を払拭しにくい状況が続くなか、英国でも金利急騰とこれに伴う財政悪化リスクには要注意
政府債務残高と10年債利回り変化幅の関係

中国：製造業利益は引き続きAI需要と資源高がけん引。7～9月期の利益率は前期を下回る
 9月の製造業総利益は前年比＋14.4％と前月（同＋9.1％）から加速。引き続きAI需要と資源高がけん引
― コンピューター・通信機器は同＋72.9％の増益。石油・石炭加工は単月赤字から一転し、同10倍超の伸び
― 他方、鉄鋼は単月赤字に転落したほか、自動車も同▲68.5％の減益。過剰生産業種は厳しい状況
 7～9月期の製造業の売上高利益率は5.0％と4～6月期（5.5％）から低下。自動車など幅広い業種で前期を下回る
― コンピューター・通信機器は7.4％で、前年（4.3％）から急速に改善。資源高で非鉄金属加工も好調

― 他方、自動車は2.5％と、前年（3.8％）から大幅に悪化。過当競争による販売価格の低下が響いた格好

 中東情勢緊迫化後、食料や燃料を中心にインフレ率が急上昇したタイとフィリピンでは、4～6月期に消費の伸びが減速

 背景に実質賃金の鈍化による購買力の低下
― 購買力低下を受け、タイは6月～11月に消費支援策（食品・日用品などの購入補助）を実施、フィリピンは政府による企
業への補助も伴いながら首都圏の最低賃金の大幅な引き上げを6月に発令。物価上昇への対応が両国の政治課題に

日本：基調的物価は2％到達も安定化には距離。日銀は利上げの影響を見極め微妙な舵取りへ
13
日銀10月会合のポイント（展望レポート・総裁会見）
（出所）日本銀行「経済・物価情勢の展望」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
 日銀は基調的物価について「2％目標と概ね整合的な水準」に評価変更も、10月会合では利上げを見送り
― 今後は物価を2％で安定化させる局面へ。金融環境は緩和的との評価を維持し、次回12月会合で追加利上げの公算大
― 経済・物価はオントラックとの評価。原油価格の想定上振れを一部反映も見通し修正は軽微
― 総裁会見では「直近で物価上振れリスクが高まったわけではない」との発言。一段の利上げ加速は喫緊の必要性なし
 利上げを通じた基調的物価への影響を見極める手がかりとして、日銀は金融環境を注視

― 銀行・企業向けアンケート調査や銀行貸出残高にみられる変化、グローバル金融市場からの影響波及には要注意
経済・物価見通し
（注） 青字＝上昇修正、赤字＝下方修正。ドバイ原油価格は1バレル105ドル程度を出発
点に70ドル程度まで下落していくと前提。飲食料品消費税率の引き下げの影響は
織り込まず
（出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
全体として修正は軽微。日銀見通し
対比でオントラックとの評価
展望レポート
• 基調的物価上昇率は2%物価目標と概ね整合的な水準
• 実質金利は上昇しているが、短期中心になお低水準。資金需要、
資産市場動向等も踏まえると金融環境は依然緩和した状態
• 名目賃金は前年並みのベースアップが実現するもとで、着実な増
加が続く見込み
• 海外の多くの中央銀行が利上げ方向に政策を転換。グローバルな
金融環境の変化がわが国の経済・物価に及ぼす影響に留意
会見中の総裁発言
• 物価上振れリスクはなおも無視できないが、1カ月前から物価上振
れリスクは大きく高まったわけではない

• 基調的物価が2%にアンカーされている度合いは極めて低い
• 金融政策が適時適切に発動され、緩和度合いを調整していくこと
で期待が安定化されていく
• 本支店ヒアリングでは、中小零細を中心に資金繰りがタイトになり
つつあるという声が聞かれ、今後の広がりを注意深くみていく
• 早めの政策対応を基本方針とするべき状況までは至っていない

日本：AI関連財の増産を背景に設備投資は今後拡大も、輸入依存の高さが成長の抑制要因に
14
 9月鉱工業生産は前月比＋2.2％と3カ月ぶりに増産。26年に入ってから増産基調を維持
― 増産の主因はAI関連。旺盛なAI関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置などの増産が鉱工業生産をけん引
― 加えて、自動車生産の持ち直しなど、熊本地震や豪雨による下振れからの回復も生産の押し上げに寄与
 AI関連を中心に増産基調の生産動向は、今後の設備投資拡大を示唆
― ただし、資本財の輸入依存度は上昇傾向。AI関連業種で設備不足感が強まるなか、資本財の供給を輸入に頼る構造
― 人手不足などの供給制約で国内メーカーの増産が追いつかず、拡大する投資需要の一部が海外に流出の可能性も

2026年・2027年 みずほ世界経済見通し
～転換点は近いのか、投資主導経済の行方を探る～
（2026年10月2日発刊）
調査部レポートのご紹介 PR
2026年10月作成
レポート
URL
レポート
二次元
コード
世界経済はどこへ向かうのか？中東危機やAI・レジリエンス関連の投資が
もたらす変化や、経済・金融市場の行方を展望
【ポイント①】
中東危機が⾧期化するなかでも、AI・レジリエンス関連の旺盛な投資需要に
支えられ、世界経済は3％台の成⾧ペースを維持
【ポイント②】
世界はインフレ・高金利の新常態へ。成⾧・負担の偏在が経済主体間の
「耐性格差」を拡大
【ポイント③】
海外主要中銀はインフレ警戒から利上げモードへ、日銀も物価上振れを警戒

### Page 1

意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 05 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國製造業維持擴張，成本壓力升高：9 月 ISM 製造業調查顯示，美國製造業連續第九個月
位於擴張區間，新訂單與就業分項均見改善，反映接單與用人需求仍有支撐。然而，價格指數
由 8 月的 71.1 升至 9 月的 77.9，能源、金屬及關稅相關成本上升，加上供應商交貨偏慢，企
業面臨更大成本壓力 。 後續須觀察企業能否轉嫁成本 ， 以及通膨黏性對聯準會政策判斷的影響 。
◼ 日本短觀顯示製造業改善、服務業略緩：日本銀行 10 月 1 日公布的 9 月短觀顯示，大型製造

業景氣判斷指數由 6 月的＋22 升至＋24，大型非製造業則由＋37 降至＋35。企業預估 12 月
兩項指數分別回落至＋21 與＋30，顯示現況仍有支撐，後續看法則偏審慎。製造業改善有助
支撐日股獲利預期，但服務業放緩及日銀政策路徑仍須持續觀察。
◼ 台灣製造業活動加速擴張，AI 需求續強：10 月 1 日公布的製造業調查顯示，台灣 9 月製造業
活動擴張速度創 2021 年 8 月以來最快，AI 相關應用需求仍是重要推力。國發會 9 月 29 日公
布的 8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與 7 月持平，領先及同時指標皆續
升。科技供應鏈景氣仍有支撐，但成長動能能否擴及其他產業，仍是觀察後續景氣的重點。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，關注財報季表現： AI 基礎建設需求未受利率環境影響，而是持續擴張，帶
動光通訊、CCL、ABF 載板 、記憶體、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持強勁成長，市場表
現由全面上漲轉向結構性分化。隨新一代 AI 伺服器放量、CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優

勢、定價能力及訂單能見度延伸至 2027 年的龍頭廠商仍可望受惠。10 月中旬起陸續公布的
第三季財報，若結果持續表現亮眼，可望為市場再添動能，屆時具 AI 供應鏈題材的台股與日
股亦可望受惠。
◼ 債券佈局聚焦 5~10 年期美國公債及投等債：雖然貨幣政策風險 下降有助於穩定長期通膨預
期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長
天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，5~10 年期債券較能兼顧票息收益與存續
期間風險管理。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公司債指數利差約 80-90 個基點，雖然利差
偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供具吸引力的絕對收益水準，因此現階段5~10 年期美國公債及
投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月
政策會議進一步升息；另一方面，澳洲8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)月增 0.4%略低
於預期，但年增 4.0%仍高於澳洲央行 2~3%的目標區間。
⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪偏升。隨著美中兩國完成新一輪談判，雙方同意延長休兵，從
原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月 ， 降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險 ， 支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續 ，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方 ，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高 ， 令人民幣由升轉貶至6.7200 上方 ； 臨近週末，
美國公布 9 月非農數據降溫，聯準會10 月升息預期下滑，美元指數回落，境外人民
幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場來看 ， 美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢 ， 不過10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假 ， 預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7300

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁 ， 帶動USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪 ， 主要原因如下 ： 一 、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.50-159.00

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，EUR/USD 震盪下
行至 1.1310，而在紐約聯儲主席威廉姆斯發表不急於再次升息的偏鴿言論後，
EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1340；週間，美國 8 月 PCE 數據低於預期，EUR/USD 一度反彈
至 1.1380，然而在全球債券拋售潮下，資金回流美元帶動美元指數突破 102 關卡，
EUR/USD 跌破 1.1300 關卡最低來至 1.1214；臨近週末，美國 9 月非農就業人數低
於預期，EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1285 後回落，終場收在 1.1253，週線下跌 138 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元地區消費者
物價調和指數(HICP)，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%、歐元區年增 3.8%，顯示能
源價格衝擊依然使通膨壓力居高不下， 市場預期歐洲央行可能在 12 月再度升息 ， 將
帶給歐元部分支撐。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%、月增 0.2%低於預
期，而 9 月非農就業人數 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人 ， 失業率則由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，
就業降溫及通膨回落降低市場對聯準會十月升息的預期，然而第二季美國 GDP 終
值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，經濟基本面持穩仍有利美元維持強勢。三、技術面而言，在
日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價在各天期均線下方並跌破 1.1300 整數關卡，月線向下穿
過季線形成死亡交叉，顯示短線空方動能較強。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈
現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.29-115.10.02 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週為提存期底 ， 繼央行上週大砍存單效應持續發酵 ， 市
場資金供給仍充裕，惟需求疲弱，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.66%~1.68%，利率高位較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投仍冷清，5 年期指標券 A15108 週三於處所成交在 1.82%，10 年
期指標券則未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價為 1.95%。 
第四季台債發行量雖偏少 ， 惟美伊和談依舊陷入僵局 ， 國際油價續處高位 ， 通
膨疑慮尚存，國際債市走勢仍偏空，預期台債殖利率短線將維持高檔盤整格
局，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.95%~2%。

### Page 3

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Structural OPM upward trend — Management expects 4Q26 to mark the 
beginning of a structural improvement in OPM, supported by rapidly scaling data 
center ASIC revenue and operating leverage. Management indicated that the OPM 
could exceed 20% in 2027. Meanwhile, selective pricing adjustments across 
traditional businesses should help offset rising supply chain costs. Beyond data 
centers, Smart Edge should benefit from new AI PCs, AI tablets, automotive 
platforms and networking applications, including continued collaboration with 
NVIDIA on next-generation AI computing products. While smartphone demand 
remains relatively subdued, management expects the expanding AI business to 
increasingly dominate MediaTek’s overall growth and earnings trajectory.
Second-Generation ASIC on Track for 1Q28 
Production, with a Second Customer 
Management expressed increasing confidence in achieving the upper end of its 15–
20% market share target for the estimated US$80bn data center AI accelerator 
ASIC market in 2027, implying approximately US$16bn in potential revenue. This 
represents a substantial increase from the revised US$2.5bn target for 2026, 
supported by strong demand visibility and continued supply chain expansion. 
Management emphasized that securing adequate foundry, packaging and other 
critical component capacity remains essential to meeting customer requirements. 
Importantly, the revenue opportunity reflects MediaT ek’s increasing participation in 
the value chain, encompassing advanced process technologies, packaging, high-
speed interconnects and custom silicon integration. Despite recent market 
concerns over AI infrastructure financing and CSP capital expenditure 
sustainability, management remains confident that AI compute demand will 
continue growing over the next several years, particularly for customized 
accelerators offering superior performance per watt and total cost of ownership.
Figure 1. Mediatek - 3Q26 results overview 
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Citi Research, Bloomberg
Management reiterated that its second AI accelerator project remains on schedule 
for high-volume production in 1Q28, despite market concerns regarding design 
complexity, advanced packaging yields and potential delays. The new accelerator is 
expected to deliver multiple times the performance per watt of the first-generation 
product, significantly improving customer TCO and strengthening Media T ek’s 
competitive positioning . Management acknowledged manufacturing challenges 
associated with its U.S. IDM packaging partner but highlighted progress in 
identifying key yield constraints and implementing corrective measures, expressing 
high confidence in further yield improvements. Importantly, Media T ek also 
confirmed a separate design win with a second, non-Chinese data center

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
4
customer, with initial revenue expected in 2028. The company is already
collaborating with its existing customer on subsequent-generation products,
although specifications are still being finalized. Management expects another year
of strong revenue growth in 2028 but declined to provide specific revenue guidance
or customer contribution estimates.
Expanding competitive advantages through advanced node

design/packaging, SerDes and NVIDIA NVLink Fusion
MediaT ek continues investing in advanced technology capabilities, including 448G
SerDes, advanced packaging, HBM integration and co-packaged optics (CPO), to
establish a competitive advantage in increasingly complex AI infrastructure
designs. Management emphasized that its differentiation comes not from
individual technologies but from integrating them into high-performance, power-
efficient and commercially viable solutions. More importantly, its partnership with

NVIDIA through NVLink Fusion enables customers to integrate custom XPUs into
NVIDIA’s rack-scale ecosystem, while its support for UALink and Ethernet-based
architectures provides greater deployment flexibility. MediaT ek is expanding
beyond individual silicon components toward subsystems and rack-level
solutions, shortening customers’ development cycles and accelerating time to
revenue. Management indicated encouraging engagement with multiple potential

customers and expects broader adoption opportunities. The 448G SerDes
development is progressing well and is expected to support both existing
accelerator programs and future customers, with management not anticipating it
to become a critical schedule bottleneck.
Structural OPM expansion trend; Reiterate Buy
Management expects 4Q26 to mark the beginning of a structural improvement in
operating profitability, supported by rapidly scaling data center ASIC revenue and

operating leverage. Although ASIC gross margins are expected to remain below
MediaT ek’s traditional corporate average, the significantly larger revenue
contribution and more favorable operating expense leverage should drive
meaningful operating margin expansion. Management indicated that the operating
margin could exceed 20% in 2027, potentially somewhat higher, compared with its
historical mid-teen level. Meanwhile, selective pricing adjustments across

traditional businesses should help offset rising supply chain costs, although these
increases are primarily intended to preserve profitability rather than expand gross
margins. Beyond data centers, Smart Edge should benefit from new AI PCs, AI
tablets, automotive platforms and networking applications, including continued
collaboration with NVIDIA on next-generation AI computing products. While
smartphone demand remains relatively subdued, management expects the

expanding AI business to increasingly dominate MediaT ek’s overall growth and
earnings trajectory.
Given MediaT ek’s better operating leverage, we lift our earnings projection for
2027 /2028 5%/5%, mainly reflecting better OPM. We maintain our Buy rating with
an unchanged TP of NT$6,800, implying 29x our 2027 /28 EPS estimates.

### Page 9

Mediatek (2454.TW)
30 October 2026 Citi Research
9
Mediatek
Company description
Mediatek (MTK) is a fabless IC company that designs SoC for smartphone,
smart TV, and digital media devices. It also develops chips for connectivity,
networking, smart home gadgets, and automotive sectors. It was founded in
1997, is listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange, and is headquartered in Taiwan.
It has sales and research subsidiaries in mainland China, Singapore, India,

the US, Japan, Korea, Denmark, and England. It primarily sells its products in
China and other emerging-market countries.
Investment strategy
We rate Mediatek shares as Buy. We expect Mediatek to ride on 5G migration
in emerging markets and market share gain of its high-end Dimensity series in
China market as well. In particular, we see strong growth potential from its AI
ASIC, NTN-satellite business in the long term. We believe solid fundamentals

and strong operating leverage will support Mediatek’s margins outlook.
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for Mediatek is based on 29x P/E to
2027 /2028 EPS, which is 1-std above its five-year average forward P/E given
the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We
believe our target PE is also justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers'
trading at an average of c32x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should

provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek as well
prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication. Despite lukewarm
smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G
migration in emerging markets and its Dimensity series should also gain
shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing
stabilizing TV and Io T demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market
on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.

Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from AI ASIC business; 2)
more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall
demand related to macroeconomic risks, especially for smartphone demand;
and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What's new? Earlier today, a fire broke out at Panasonic's (covered by Kazuo
Yoshikawa) CCL factory in Hsinchu, Taiwan. The fire was extinguished before noon
(see Reuters' report), but Panasonic has yet to issue an official statement in
response to the incident.
Our view: Our checks indicate that this plant has monthly capacity of ~200K sheets

and primarily produces M6/7 and some lower-end BT substrate CCLs. Panasonic also
operates CCL sites in Fukushima, Japan; Guangzhou and Suzhou, China; and
Ayutthaya, Thailand, which remains under construction. Most high-end M7/8 CCL is
produced in Fukushima, while the China sites supply much of its mainstream CCL.
Our supply chain checks suggest Panasonic is reallocating capacity toward high-end
CCL for AI applications – particularly a key US hyperscaler’s in-house ASICs, as high-

end CCL supply remains tight. Although the Hsinchu plant is immaterial to group
capacity, the fire could accelerate this shift, potentially driving near-term customer
order overflow to Taiwanese CCL competitors.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
A fire at Panasonic's CCL factory in Hsinchu may push near-term orderflow to
Taiwanese competitors.
Within our coverage, we see EMC as most likely to receive orders, followed by
TUC and Shengyi Tech (EW).
EMC is also a high-end CCL supplier to Panasonic’s key ASIC customer.
Valuations are attractive for EMC and TUC, and we remain OW on both.

November 4, 2026 04:01 PM GMT

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

기업분석 2025.09.28 www.daishin.com

Issue & News 
SK하이닉스  
(000660) 
 소음보다 방향성에 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

### Page 1

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180 억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK 을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.

Document 281: Apollo拟豪掷750亿美元采购AI芯片，规模远超任何一家Neocloud

Source PDF: all report pdf/Apollo拟豪掷750亿美元采购AI芯片，规模远超任何一家Neocloud.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

私募巨头 Apollo 正以 750 亿美元的惊⼈押注重塑 AI 芯⽚融资格局 ——350 亿美元购买⾕歌
TPU 租赁给 Anthropic ， 400 亿美元购⼊英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚租赁给 SpaceX 。这⼀规模
远超最⼤ neocloud CoreWeave 的 270 亿美元，使 Apollo 跻⾝仅次于亚⻢逊、微软、⾕歌的
全球芯⽚储备第四极。
摘要：
私募巨头 Apollo Global Management 正以前所未有的规模押注 AI 芯⽚市场，其两笔待完成的芯
⽚交易合计⾦额⾼达 750 亿美元，不仅远超最⼤ neocloud CoreWeave ⾃ 2023 年以来累计 270 亿
美元的芯⽚融资规模，更是排名第⼆的竞争对⼿的逾 10 倍。
据 The Information 最新报道，SpaceX 宣布将租⽤价值 400 亿美元的英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚，⽽
Apollo 将通过旗下⼦公司购⼊这批芯⽚后再租赁给 SpaceX 。此前， Apollo 已于今年 6 ⽉宣布联

合 Broadcom 与 Blackstone ，为其购买⾕歌张量处理器（ TPU ）并租赁给 Anthropic 的交易安排了
350 亿美元融资。
⼀旦上述交易完成，在 AI 芯⽚持有规模上，Apollo 将仅次于亚⻢逊、微软、⾕歌等超⼤规模云
服务商，成为全球芯⽚储备最雄厚的⾮超⼤规模玩家，彻底改变 AI 基础设施的竞争格局。 Apollo
在这些交易中通过撮合交易及管理融资基⾦赚取费⽤。
两笔巨型交易奠定芯⽚霸主地位
Apollo 当前的芯⽚布局由两笔核⼼交易构成。
其⼀是今年 6 ⽉宣布的 350 亿美元交易：Apollo 联合 Broadcom 与 Blackstone ，购买⾕歌 TPU 并租
赁给 AI 初创公司 Anthropic 。其⼆是上周随 SpaceX 公告浮出⽔⾯的 400 亿美元交易：Apollo 通过
⼦公司购⼊英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚，再租赁给⻢斯克旗下的 SpaceX 。
两笔交易合计 750 亿美元，使 Apollo 在芯⽚融资领域的体量远超任何⼀家 neocloud 。据 The

Information 汇编的数据， CoreWeave ⾃ 2023 年以来通过芯⽚抵押融资累计筹资 270 亿美元，位
居 neocloud 之⾸；其后依次为 Valor （ 54 亿美元）、 Nscale （ 49 亿美元）、 IREN （ 39 亿美元）、
Lambda （ 15 亿美元）、 Crusoe （ 11 亿美元）、 Nebius （ 7.75 亿美元）及 Sharon AI （ 3.56 亿美
元）。
值得注意的是，上述统计仅涵盖以 GPU 和 TPU 为抵押的融资，不包括各家 neocloud 为运营融资
⽽发⾏的可转换债券及信贷额度。此外，上周另有⼀笔由花旗集团、美国银⾏与 Blackstone 联合
安排、规模达 630 亿美元的交易 —— ⽤于为 Anthropic 采购 Broadcom 芯⽚提供融资 —— 亦未纳⼊
上述⽐较，原因是各⽅未披露这批芯⽚的实际持有⽅。
芯⽚融资模式： SPV 结构的规模化复制
Apollo 所采⽤的芯⽚融资结构，脱胎于 CoreWeave 与 Blackstone 在 2023 年共同开创的特殊⽬的

载体（ SPV ）模式。在这⼀安排下， neocloud 与其⾦融合作⽅共同设⽴ SPV ，由 SPV 发⾏债务
以购买芯⽚，芯⽚终端⽤⼾⽀付的租⾦则⽤于偿还债务。
Apollo 的操作逻辑与此⼀脉相承，但规模更为庞⼤。与许多 neocloud 不同的是， Apollo 并未额外
投⼊资本建设⽤于存放芯⽚的数据中⼼，其商业模式更为聚焦 —— 通过购买芯⽚、出租给终端
⽤⼾，赚取交易撮合费⽤及基⾦管理费。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
超⼤规模云服务商则⽆需借助 SPV 结构，可直接通过⾃由现⾦流、股权发⾏或传统公司债完成
芯⽚采购，这也是 Apollo 与超⼤规模云服务商之间的本质区别。
保险资本驱动 Apollo 转型
Apollo 的芯⽚豪赌，折射出这家⽼牌私募机构过去⼗年的深刻转型。 Apollo 由亿万富翁 Leon
Black 联合创⽴，成⽴已逾 36 年，早年以杠杆收购闻名，其中包括 2008 年斥资近 300 亿美元收购
赌场巨头哈拉斯娱乐。
过去⼗年间， Apollo ⼤规模吸纳来⾃保险公司的资本。保险资⾦偏好收益率⾼于投资级债券、
但信⽤评级仍维持投资级的资产类别，⽽芯⽚融资交易恰好契合这⼀需求。这⼀资本结构的转
变，为 Apollo 在 AI 基础设施领域的⼤规模布局提供了充裕的弹药。

芯⽚融资对 Apollo ⽽⾔是⼀个全新的业务⽅向，但其背后的逻辑与传统私募信贷如出⼀辙：以资
产为抵押，提供结构化融资，赚取稳定的利差收益。随着 AI 算⼒需求持续膨胀， Apollo 正将这⼀
逻辑推向新的量级。

Document 308: Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-07).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

### Page 1

Muted iPhone 18 Pro Cycle and Rising Memory Costs

[Table_Rank]

Maintained  Hold  
 Target price US$334

[Table_Summary] Lower TP to $334, Hold rating: Driven by investors being away from AI themes, Apple share 
price outperformed Nasdaq by 7.2% in the past one month. However, we continue to expect 
muted cycle of iPhone 18 P/PM with constrained volume of iPhone Duo. Additionally, memory 
cost hike will last longer than expected, driven by our view of tight DRAM/NAND supply, and 
we expect cost hikes throughout CY2027. For hardware, we still expect the iPhone 20 (2H27) 
to be a strong product cycle, thanks to new design of display, followed by Apple’s early move 
to TSMC ’s A14 in 2H28 (suggesting only 2 -years at 2nm). Additionally, Apple Intelligence 
remains the key to watch. Yet, the share price trades at 35x FY27E P/E, suggesting the stock 
isn’t cheap. By factoring in results, guidance and lower shipments/margins, we revise FY27E 
EPS by -10% and revise TP from $369 to $334, still based on 35x FY27E. 
Dec-Q margin guidance below expect ations: Apple delivered solid Sep t-Q results, with 
revenue of $114.5bn (+12% YoY) and diluted EPS of $2.1 (+14% YoY), both ahead of 
consensus. For Dec-Q, mgmt. guided to total revenue growth of 7 –10% YoY (vs. consensus 
of +6.5%), Services growth of 8 –9%, and gross margin of 45 –46%. The revenue outlook is 
constructive to Street expectations, though the margin guidance and Services deceleration 
were disappointing, reflecting cost hikes and mix headwinds.  
The iPhone product cycle mixed, with challenges in FY2027: Mgmt sees a strong start of 
iPhone Duo but remains supply constrained. Hence, we continue to expect the EMS build plan 
of 6.5m of iPhone Duo by end-2026 with limited upside, while the mainstream iPhone 18 P/PM 
to be 74.5m. As said in our earlier report of iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max Lukewarm on Sept 15, we 
believe the muted demand will weigh on CY1H27 EMS builds. Margin wise, despite p ricing 
actions taken earlier, margin will likely be under pressure, as the CEO Ternus characterized 
current memory pricing as “unprecedented,” with higher costs in December and beyond.  
Apple positioning the rebuilt Siri AI as a core differentiator: The CEO Ternus emphasized 
that the new Siri is deeply integrated and privacy -preserving and is fielding ~2.5bn requests 
per day. From a financial perspective, CFO Parekh noted that these Si ri AI costs are 
embedded in the December margin outlook, suggesting near-term gross-margin compression 
alongside the memory cost up-cycle.  
Risks: 1) weak demand, 2) competition, 3) product delays, 4) geo-tension.

### Page 2

Earnings Forecast  
Solid September Quarter Results  
Apple delivered a solid September-quarter, with revenue of $114.5bn (+12% YoY) and diluted EPS of 
$2.10 (+14% YoY), both ahead of consensus, reaching a record fiscal year of $479bn in revenue. Gross 
margin came in at 47.9%, aided by approximately one percentage point of tariff refund benefits (and a 
$0.05 EPS tailwind).  
iPhone revenue reached a September-quarter record of $57.6bn (+17.5% YoY) likely driven by higher 
ASPs, Mac rose a strong 24% to $10.8bn likely on edge-AI, Services set at $31.6bn (+9.8% YoY) which 
was below expectations, while iPad declined 10% to $6.2bn and Wearables fell 8% to $8.3bn. The 
company returned $28bn to shareholders. 
December Quarter Guidance Slightly Ahead in Revenue but Shy in Margin  
For the December quarter, mgmt. guided to total revenue growth of 7 –10% YoY ( vs. consensus of 
+6.5%), Services growth of 8–9%, and gross margin of 45–46%. Operating expenses are expected at 
$20.4–20.8bn, OI&E around $300 million, and the tax rate near 17%. The revenue outlook is 
constructive relative to prior Street expectations, though the margin range and Services deceleration 
reflect known cost and mix headwinds.

### Page 5

Valuation and Recommendation  
Hold with TP of $334 
We rate Apple (AAPL US) Hold and set our TP at $334 which is based on 35x our FY27E EPS estimate. 
We believe it is important to capture the 2H26 -3Q27’s iPhone 18 product cycle, given high market 
interest. We adopt P/E methodology as we believe the market now mainly focuses on the company’s 
earnings outlook, and we usually apply P/E method to downstream firms.  
We believe Apple’s ecosystem will remain strong and associated revenue durability is high. This is 
backed by Apple's installed base growth, double digit YoY growth in services. However, the market 
would wait for visibility for its Apple Intelligence. Meanwhile, the market will need more visibility on the 
higher-high retail prices for the iPhone 18 series due to the rising cost over tariff and 2nm/memories. 
That said, we expect its earnings growth to be  high-teens to 20% YoY in FY26E-FY28E, from single 
digit YoY in FY22-FY24. Considering >30x one-year P/E, the stock doesn’t look particularly cheap.

### Page 1

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH 
EARNINGS AGGREGATES 
STOXX 600 Earnings Outlook provides analysis and commentary on aggregate earnings estimate revisions, growth rates and valuations.

Analyst: Tajinder Dhillon, CFA                                         November 5, 2026 
tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com

STOXX 600 EARNINGS OUTLOOK  
EUROPEAN AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS

 Third quarter earnings are expected to increase 25.0% from Q3 2026. Excluding the Energy sector, earnings 
are expected to increase 9.5%.

 Third quarter revenue is expected to increase 11.9% from Q3 2026. Excluding the Energy sector, revenues are 
expected to increase 6.9%.

 212 companies in the STOXX 600 have reported earnings to date for Q3 2026. Of these, 58.0% reported results 
exceeding analyst estimates.

 257 companies in the STOXX 600 have reported revenue to date for Q3 2026. Of these, 64.2% reported revenue 
exceeding analyst estimates.

 During the week of November 9, 40 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

Through November 5, 212 companies in the STOXX 600 Index reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these, 58% reported 
earnings above analyst estimates. In a typical quarter (sin ce 2012), 54% of companies beat estimates and 39% miss 
estimates.

In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.2% above estimates, which is below the 5.9% long-term 
(since 2012) average surprise factor.

Through November 5, 257 companies in the STOXX 600 Index reported revenues for Q3 2026. Of these, 64.2% 
exceeded analyst expectations. In a typical quarter (since 2012), 58% of companies beat estimates and 42% miss 
estimates.

In aggregate, companies are reporting revenues that are 1.5% below estimates, which is below the 1.3% long-term 
(since 2012) average surprise factor.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the STOXX 600 fo r Q3 2026 is 25%. The STOXX 600 expects to see share-
weighted earnings of €164.4B in Q3 2026, compared to shar e-weighted earnings of €131.6B (based on the year-ago 
earnings of the current constituents) in Q3 2025.

Eight of the ten sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to Q3 2025. The Energy sector 
has the highest earnings growth rate for the quarter, wh ile the Real Estate has the weakest anticipated growth 
compared to Q3 2025.

The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (153 .4%) of any sector. It is expected to earn €35.7B in Q3 
2026, compared to earnings of €14.1B in Q3 2025.

The Real Estate sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (-69.6%) of any sector. It is expected to earn €0.9B in Q3 
2026, compared to earnings of €3.1B in Q3 2025.

The estimated revenue growth rate for the STOXX 600 for Q3 2026 is 11.9%. The STOXX 600 is expected to earn 
revenues of €1581.6B in Q3 2026, compared to €1413.6B in Q3 2025.

Eight of the ten sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in revenues relative to Q3 2025. The Energy 
sector has the highest revenue growth rate for the quarter, while the Consumer Cyclicals has the weakest anticipated 
growth compared to Q3 2025.

The Energy sector has the highest revenue growth rate (39.4%) of any sector. It is expected to generate sales of 
€302.7B in Q3 2026, compared to sales of €217.2B in Q3 2025.

The Consumer Cyclicals sector has the lowest revenue growth rate (-1.2%) of any sector. It is expected to generate 
sales of €196.8B in Q3 2026, compared to sales of €199.1B in Q3 2025.

Analysts expect positive earnings growth from 14 of th e 16 countries represented in the STOXX 600 index. Poland 
(86.5%) and Norway (85.6%) have the highest estimated earnings growth rates, while Germany (-7.7%) and Ireland (-
3.5%) have the lowest estimated growth.

AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
Note: The estimate revision numbers below are an aggregate of the total number of earnings estimate 
revisions for the Fiscal Year 1 period for all companies in the STOXX 600 over the previous seven days. 
Up revisions represent the total number of estimates for Fiscal Year 1 submitted in the past seven days 
that are higher than the previous estimates for Fiscal Year 1. Down revisions represent the total number 
of estimates for Fiscal Year 1 submitted in the past seven days of that are lower than the previous 
estimates for Fiscal Year 1.

Q3 2026 – Q3 2027: EARNINGS GROWTH

The estimated earnings growth rates for the STOXX 600 for Q3 2026 through Q3 2027 are 25%, 39.1%, 14.9%, -3.7% 
and 7.1%, respectively. For sector-level growth rates, see Exhibits 2C-6C in this report.

EARNINGS CALENDAR

There are 40 STOXX 600 companies with quarterly earnings esti mates scheduled to report quarterly earnings during 
the week of November 9, 2026.

THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

than a year ago. The Aerospace & Defense (-39.6%) and Passenger Airlines (-29.8%) sub-industries have the lowest 
earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate improves to 30.5%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

Charts of the week: No capitulation

A hotter-than-expected US inflation report triggered a pullback in risk assets, but there are few signs of capitulation so far 
US core and supercore inflation; US inflation breadth*    Our ‘Fear and Greed’ indicator

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *share of US consumer inflation components rising more than 3%

Editorial 
Staying defensive and diversified  
Strategy summary:  Two weeks ago, we turned defensive 
towards equities, locking in semiconductor equity gains amid 
elevated bond volatility, narrowing market breadth and political 
uncertainty before the US mid -term elections. The near -term 
outlook reflected a less favourable risk-reward balance. Since 
we turned defensive , the Nasdaq 100 index has recorded its 
biggest weekly decline in three months as September’s hotter-
than-expected US inflation report briefly lifted bond yields. 
We have extended our defensive stance this week, 
downgrading US Communication Services equities to Neutral 
and upgrading US Healthcare to Overweight. We also closed 
our opportunistic bullish Communication Services idea and 
initiated a bullish idea on Healthcare, where earnings visibility 
and defensive characteristics appear more attractive. In fixed 
income, we see opportunities in US 10-year government bonds. 
We await clearer AI profit margins and spending guidance, 
broader earnings growth or lower bond and equity market 
volatility before turning positive on equities. Until then, 
diversification and disciplined entry levels remain important. 
Hot US inflation triggers equity pullback; not hot enough 
for an October Fed hike:  Risk assets pulled back after 
September’s hotter-than-expected inflation data (headline 3.7% 
y/y; core 2.6% y/y). Higher gasoline and diesel prices were the 
main drivers, but passthrough appeared in airline fares, used 
cars and transportation services, showing pressures were not 
confined to energy. Almost half of inflation components are still 
rising above 3%, a measure recently cited by Fed Chair Warsh. 
Producer inflation also beat estimates. The reports revive the 
possibility of an October Fed rate hike, but we expect a divided 
Fed to weigh it against September’s soft jobs report and 
narrowly hold this month. A December hike appears more likely 
if inflation remains firm . Although Fed Chair Warsh did not 
comment on policy at an IMF speech this week, New York Fed 
chief Williams said he saw no need to hike in 2027. Our base 
case remains two further hikes by June 2027. 
Limited equity pullback so far.  Equities fell sharply after the 
US inflation report, but the pullback is limited. The Nasdaq 100 
is 4.5% below its record high, while the S&P 500 remains close 
to all -time highs despite the latest decline. The S&P 500 
volatility index (VIX) has risen from below 15 to 1 8.2, still well 
under the 25 –30 range typically associated with capitulation 
and forced selling. Meanwhile, US government bond volatility 
(MOVE) remains elevated after surging since September.  Our 
stock-bond quant model turned more negativ e (-2) this week, 
signalling elevated equity volatility in the near term.  
When to turn more constructive on stocks?  We would turn 
more constructive when capitulation signals align: the Nasdaq 
100 approaches a 10% drawdown, the VIX rises to 25 –30 and 
our ‘Fear and Greed’ index  enters “extreme fear”. Greater 
confidence would also require easing concerns about AI 
spending and technology firms’ exposure to neocloud revenue, 
lower bond volatility and our stock-bond model turning positive 
again. Until these conditions emerge, volatility may remain 
elevated, favouring a defensive equity stance. Political risk 
should ease after the US mid -term elections. We continue to 
seek selective opportunities following excessive corrections. 
US financials remain a preferred sector after Q3 earnings:  
US large banks’ earnings were mixed, but underlying trends 
remain encouraging. Healthy loan and deposit growth supports 
net interest income, while resilient consumer spending, wealth 
management activity and a solid M&A and refinancing pipeline 
strengthen the outlook. Credit risks appear manageable . We 
remain constructive and favour stronger, diversified franchises. 
US government bonds offer value as yields spike:  The 
reversal in US government bond yields after their initial inflation-
driven spike suggests limited further upside. The 10 -year yield 
briefly reached 5.32% before slumping as falling equities 
prompted short-covering. We expect a near -term 5.00–5.25% 
range and view moves above 5.25% as attractive entry points 
for our opportunistic 10-year government bond idea. Structural 
pressures, including term premium and increasing supply to 
fund fiscal deficits and AI investments, prevent us from turning 
outright bullish on longer maturities. We, therefore, continue to 
prefer 3–7-year maturities in broader bond allocations.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions 
  How does the September 2026 US Consumer Price Index 
(CPI) report alter your view on the USD and bond yields?   
Our view: The hotter-than-expected CPI print raises the odds of 
an October Fed hike, though we still expect a hold followed by 
a rate hike in December. In the near term, we maintain our view 
that the US 10 -year government bond yield will range between 
5.00% and 5.25%, while  the USD will remain supported as 
markets reassess the likelihood of further Fed hikes. Additional 
gains would depend partly on markets pricing a faster or more 
prolonged tightening cycle, while clearer signs of an 
approaching policy rate peak could see the USD ease back. 
Rationale: September US consumer inflation  came in above 
consensus across the board – headline CPI rose 3.7% y/y (vs. 3.6% 
estimate), and core CPI rose 2.6% y/y (vs. 2.5% estimate) – marking 
the fastest headline acceleration in five months. This shifts the 
balance of risks modestly hawkish . Unsurprisingly, the market -
implied probability of an October Fed hike rose over 50% overnight 
but closed the day at 32.7% – 9ppt higher than Tuesday’s close. 
We believe a split Fed will weigh this inflation report against the soft 
September jobs report and narrowly vote to keep rates on hold this 
month. Our base case remains for two more Fed hikes  by June 
2027. A December rate hike  now looks more like a done deal, 
especially as persistently higher oil prices keep headline and core 
inflation elevated for at least the next few months. Any re-escalation 
of the Middle East conflict after the  US mid-term elections would 
exert pressure on the Fed, although we believe Republicans would  
show greater restraint in US foreign action if they suffer steep Senate 
losses, which looks increasingly likely. 
The intra-day price action in US government bonds was instructive. 
The US 10-year yield briefly spiked to 5.32% on the print before 
closing materially lower, as pre -existing bearish positioning was 
unwound and Fed speakers (Barkin, Goolsbee) stopped well short 
of signalling an imminent move. This dynamic reinforces our view 
that the technical selling pressure driving yields towards the upper 
end of our range is fading. Structural drivers – term premium, fiscal 
supply concerns and ‘higher for longer’ Fed repricing – remain intact 
and keep us from turning outright bullish on duration. However, our 
opportunistic bullish idea in US 10-year government bonds initiated 
at September-end remains, as we continue to view spikes in US 10-
year yields over 5.25% as a good risk-reward entry. From a longer-
term portfolio standpoint, we continue to favour the 3-7-year tenor. 
On FX, elevated US government bond yields should sustain the 
USD’s interest rate appeal. However, expected hikes alone will not 
drive further appreciation. Stronger data or firmer Fed guidance 
would provide fresh support, while evidence that tightening is 
nearing completion would temper the USD’s strength. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are your key takeaways from US bank earnings? 
Our view: Q3 2026 earnings results from major US banks have 
been mixed, but underlying trends remain encouraging. Healthy 
loan and deposit growth, resilient consumer spending and 
wealth management activity continue to support the sector. 
Credit risks remain manageable. We remain constructive on the 
US financial sector, favouring stronger, diversified franchises. 
Rationale: Healthy loan and deposit growth continue to support net 
interest income (NII), hinting that the US economy remains resilient. 
Capital markets revenue grew year -on-year (y/y) , but moderated 
sequentially after a very strong Q2 2026, although performance 
varied across banks. Management commentary points to a healthy 
deal pipeline , supported by M&A activity and debt refinancing 
demand, although execution will depend on market conditions. 
Provisioning and net charge -off trends were mixed across banks, 
with no clear signs of broad deterioration in credit quality. Firmer 
US inflation raises the risk of further monetary tightening. While 
higher rates can support asset yields, they can also increase 
funding costs, pressure borrowers and weigh on securities ’ 
valuations. We will continue to monitor lending demand, credit 
costs, NII trends, capital markets momentum and capital returns.  
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Can Big Tech’s earnings guidance sustain confidence in 
the AI investment cycle? 
Our view: We expect Big Tech’s AI spending to remain elevated, 
supporting our constructive AI capex outlook. However, we 
await guidance on the pace of capex growth and the 
sustainability of profit margins. 
Rationale: Semiconductor leaders’ latest company guidance points 
to supply constraints extending through 2028, supporting continued 
capacity expansion for compute . However, while AI capex is still 
likely to rise, y/y growth is expected to moderate  from c.100% in 
Q3 2026 to c.20% in Q3 2027. This normalisation in growth could 
raise concerns about the AI investment cycle’s durability.  
The Nasdaq-100 Index has rallied alongside accelerating AI capex. 
Slower capex growth is likely to moderate semiconductor revenue 
growth, while hyperscalers continue to incur depreciation costs on 
past investments. The slower growth is already partially reflected in 
the Nasdaq -100’s valuation de -rating this year. While we remain 
constructive on the AI investment theme, we prefer to stay 
prudent in the near term and await Big Tech’s guidance for greater 
visibility into spending plans in H2 2027 and beyond. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would France face a credit rating downgrade by rating 
agencies this month? What might be the market implications? 
Our view: A rating downgrade is unlikely unless France’s 2027 
budget passes the National Assembly very soon with material 
changes to the proposal. We see French government bond 
yields remaining elevated relative to German Bund yields. 
Rationale: The French National Assembly began debating France’s 
2027 draft budget on 13 October 2026, with the first vote scheduled 
for 20 October. Near-term budget approval appears unlikely, as 
opposition parties have tabled motions to reject the proposal. The 
French Parliament has up to 70 days from the budget’s submission 
to complete the legislative process, although  its final adoption and 
the resolution of political challenges could extend into early 2027.  
Last October, two of the three major rating agencies downgraded 
France by one notch to the high-single-A category  with stable 
outlooks. The remaining agenc y rated France in the low-AA 
category with a negative outlook. As negative outlooks are typically 
resolved within 12 -18 months, agenc ies have time to await fiscal 
clarity before acting. We believe downgrade risks are largely priced 
in after the recent rise in yields, but volatility is likely to persist amid 
fiscal uncertainty and the approaching presidential election. Banks 
and domestically-focused issuers would face the greatest near-term 
pressure. However, we would view wider spreads as an opportunity 
to add exposure, particularly to French banks’ subordinated 
debt, given their stronger balance sheets and the fact that France 
contributes less than 50% of major French banks’ overall revenue. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Japanese banks? 
Our view: We are positive on Japanese banks despite the recent 
market correction, as the structural drivers of improving 
profitability and shareholder returns remain intact . While 
political risk and shifting BoJ rate hike odds could sustain near-
term volatility, we see the recent pullback as an opportunity to 
build selective exposure to Japanese bank stocks. 
Rationale: Japan’s gradual monetary policy normalisation  
should support banks’ NII as floating-rate loans reprice and maturing 
assets are reinvested at higher yields. Healthy corporate lending 
demand, improving capital efficiency and continued share buybacks 
provide further support . While uncertainty over the pace of BoJ 
tightening could weigh on sentiment, we see gradual interest rate 
normalisation as supportive of medium-term bank profitability.  
Key risks  include rising deposit costs, mark -to-market losses on 
bond portfolios and a disorderly increase in Japanese government 
bond yields. We continue to favour well-capitalised banks  with 
strong domestic deposit franchises and improving returns on equity. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Could NZD/USD rebound from its one-year trough? If so, 
how far could it recover?  
Our view: New Zealand’s improving domestic conditions, 
persistent inflation and technical support provide a basis for 
NZD/USD’s initial recovery towards the 0.57 level, although 
elevated US bond yields could slow its progress. 
Rationale: In Q3, New Zealand’s  seasonally-adjusted business 
confidence reading rose to a net 40% of firms expecting the economy 
to improve, up from net 14% in Q2 . Hiring intentions improved, and 
filled jobs rose 0.2% m/m in August. These developments suggest 
domestic conditions remain supportive for the NZD.  
New Zealand’s above-target inflation keeps further RBNZ tightening 
in focus. The 22 October consumer inflation data release and the 
28 October RBNZ policy meeting are key catalysts: persistent price 
pressures a nd improving demand are expected to prompt firmer 
policy guidance and strengthen the NZD’s interest-rate appeal. 
NZD/USD’s position near the lower boundary of its downtrend 
channel suggests scope for a technical rebound. We see 0.57 as 
the first recovery hurdle; a sustained break  above this level  would 
strengthen the case for further gains. Firmer US  consumer inflation 
remains a headwind, making a gradual recovery more plausible 
than a sharp reversal.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  Can the proposed Russia -Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire ’ 
contain oil prices as Middle East risks rise? 
Our view: We expect escalating Middle East tensions to keep oil 
prices elevated . We  maintain our  three- and 12 -month West 
Texas Intermediate (WTI) oil price forecasts at USD 100/bbl and 
USD 80/bbl, respectively, and continue to favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a portfolio hedge 
against persistent inflation risks. 
Rationale: The proposed Russia-Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire’ still 
looks some way off. In our view, even if an agreement is reached, it 
would do little to ease the supply risks building in the Middle East. 
Gulf oil exports have recovered to above 80% of pre-conflict levels 
in recent weeks, helping moderate the pace of price increases. 
However, the recovery remains vulnerable to disruptions, particularly 
following the latest Houthi attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure . 
Diesel and jet fuel remain under the most pressure , with Gulf 
exports of these fuels in September  at only around 60% of pre -
conflict levels. A sustained disruption to major Saudi production or 
export infrastructure remains the principal upside risk to oil prices. 
—  Anthony Naab, Investment Strategist

### Page 3

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
US-China talks, a possible Trump-Xi-Putin trilateral meeting, and any signs of a China-Japan diplomatic
thaw will be critical watchpoints. The priorities of the summit between representatives of 21 Asia-Pacific
economies are set to include efforts to boost innovation, digital connectivity, supply-chain resilience and

support for an open multilateral trading system. During the summit, presidents Trump and Xi are expected to
meet for the third time this year. This meeting will provide signals on the state of China-US talks, including the
operationalisation of new bilateral trade and investment boards, and on the likelihood of another extension of
the Busan trade truce beyond January 2027. President Trump has also indicated he is open to a trilateral US-
China-Russia meeting during the summit, possibly covering nuclear arms control and the Russia-Ukraine war.

Investors will also closely watch whether Japanese Prime Minister Takaichi and President Xi meet on the
sidelines, following reports that Tokyo is hoping to arrange a meeting. However, the bar for a formal
breakthrough will likely remain high. As we noted in a report earlier this year, a full normalisation of relations
and an easing of Chinese economic restrictions – which include rare-earth export controls – may require a
lengthy period of diplomatic re-engagement and backchannel diplomacy.

A divided government could mean less change at home but more abroad. Third-party polling and betting
markets forecast Democrat control of the House, and an increasing probability of flipping the Senate. 80% of
respondents in our monthly poll now say living costs will influence their vote – the most since April. For
markets, a divided government could mean less domestic legislative change, but not necessarily a more
predictable US foreign policy. Losing Congress could leave theadministration’s geopolitical and geoeconomic

toolkit largely intact.
In our view, weapons stockpiles, inflationary pressures and long-end yields may matter more than Congress
in constraining theadministration’s choices in 2027 and 2028. A Democratic House could dial up oversight of
the administration, launch investigations, force votes on War Powers Resolutions, and use appropriations bills
to press for restrictions on departmental funding. This may generate a steady stream of attention-grabbing

headlines, but whether it decisively affects the event-driven risk that matters for markets (e.g. blocking a tariff
or preventing a limited military strike) remains in doubt. If domestic policymaking becomes more challenging,
foreign policy could become a top priority for the Trump administration.
In the spotlight for November 2026: US midterms and APEC Summit
US midterm elections (3 November)
APEC Economic Leaders’ Meeting in Shenzhen (18-19 November)

### Page 4

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
NewZealand’s general election is shaping up as a close and fragmented contest. Both the ruling National
and opposition Labour parties are polling at historically low levels, and smaller parties will likely determine
the next government. In a closely contested vote, investors could see coalition uncertainty and a wider

range of possible outcomes for tax, spending, infrastructure, energy and migration policy.
The National party is campaigning on a platform that would broadly preserve the current emphasis on
fiscal restraint, lower regulation and private-sector investment. A Labour-led government could place
greater weight on public services, housing and climate policy, alongside a proposed capital-gains tax on
investment property. However, because any government will rely on minor parties for survival, the final

policy mix will depend heavily on post-election negotiations.
The meeting will be a test of the EU’s ability to translate the competitiveness agenda into practical
reforms. The focus will be on Europe’s ability to restore competitiveness, mobilize investment and
maintain fiscal discipline. The Eurogroup brings together Euro-area finance ministers and is expected to
consider macroeconomic and fiscal developments, energy market pressures and progress towards the

Savings and Investments Union. ECOFIN brings together all EU finance ministers, and will focus on
financial integration, banking sector competitiveness, and economic coordination. To watch will be
tensions between fiscal consolidation and the need to fund defence, energy security and strategic
investment, alongside progress on integratingEurope’s fragmented capital markets and banking sectors. A
budget-focused Council meeting follows on 10 November.

Implementation of existing commitments, rather than new policy pledges, is expected to be the primary
focus of the next climate summit in Antalya. Building on the shift towards implementation at COP30,
COP31 will focus on translating existing climate commitments into practical action, including through
investment, financing and partnerships. For investors, the key watchpoint will be whether the summit can
deliver tangible commitments that translate existing plans into binding funding pledges for critical

infrastructure resilience, scaled-up renewable energy output, and climate finance.
Key events, issues and themes to watch in November 2026
Listed in chronological order
